## [ctx:sheel-mohnot-better-tomorrow-ventures-0587]

Kind: investment_approach · Source: full:b96ea26fcd774478dd26546a

Mohnot says his team committed around September to lead a company's seed round before its fund had closed.

> Now part of the reason we did the first close is we had a company that we had to invest in. We had committed to this company in like September. We said, we're going to lead your seed round. But we actually didn't have a fund at that point.

## [ctx:sheel-mohnot-better-tomorrow-ventures-0597]

Kind: company_relationship · Source: full:3350945d6acc45274d12367f

Mohnot says the team needed to close quickly to fund the company and identifies that company as Unit.

> close as soon as possible so we could fund that company. And I believe, I think you, it's the company was Unit, a banking as a

## [ctx:sheel-mohnot-better-tomorrow-ventures-0598]

Kind: dated_event · Source: full:3350945d6acc45274d12367f

Mohnot says that during the 2020 disruption the team reduced its stated fund target from $60 million to approximately $30–40 million.

> So we're like, okay, we were shooting for, at that point we were shooting for a $60 million fund. We said, you know what? We sent a letter to LPs saying, you know what? We're going to go for like 30 to 40.

## [ctx:sheel-mohnot-better-tomorrow-ventures-0599]

Kind: investment_approach · Source: full:3350945d6acc45274d12367f

Mohnot says the fund ultimately reached 75 from a target of 60 and reduced some requested LP allocations.

> was 60. We ended up going to 75. And even at the 75, we had to push people down from what they wanted to invest.

## [ctx:sheel-mohnot-better-tomorrow-ventures-0600]

Kind: dated_event · Source: full:3350945d6acc45274d12367f

Mohnot says Fund Two was raised in December 2021 through a process lasting only one month.

> So I would say for fund two, now fund two we raised in December 2021. The whole process only took a month.

## [ctx:sheel-mohnot-better-tomorrow-ventures-0601]

Kind: investment_approach · Source: full:3350945d6acc45274d12367f

Mohnot contrasts a roughly year-long first-fund raise with Fund Two, for which he says demand exceeded available capacity.

> So it took us about a year from start to finish. And fund two was very different because there really was like much more demand than for our fund than we had space.

## [ctx:sheel-mohnot-better-tomorrow-ventures-0602]

Kind: investment_approach · Source: full:3350945d6acc45274d12367f

Mohnot says each LP represents no more than 10% of the fund, limiting dependence on any single investor in a subsequent raise.

> are 10 % or less of our fund. And it makes us feel good now in a market where if any one LP doesn't come into our next fund, because you know, circumstances changed, it doesn't, it's fine for us. We have other folks who want to fill those gaps.

## [ctx:sheel-mohnot-better-tomorrow-ventures-0603]

Kind: investment_approach · Source: full:3350945d6acc45274d12367f

Mohnot says the team capped some Fund Two LP commitments at 10% despite requests to invest larger amounts.

> We actually pushed folks down to the 10%. So some folks had wanted to do larger checks and we pushed them down.

## [ctx:sheel-mohnot-better-tomorrow-ventures-0604]

Kind: investment_approach · Source: full:3350945d6acc45274d12367f

Mohnot says the fund's LP advisory committee consists of five large investors: one family office and four funds of funds.

> There's five of our like biggest investors. They're one family office and then the others are fund of funds.

## [ctx:sheel-mohnot-better-tomorrow-ventures-0961]

## [ctx:sheel-mohnot-better-tomorrow-ventures-0962]

Kind: investment_approach · Source: full:15a2358d269edb79cdf85ca8

Pai reports that Mohnot discussed fund sizing, multistage seed investors, follow-on strategy, emerging-markets pricing, VC value-add, secondary sales, and GP-commit alignment on 20VC.

> Sheel is forthright with his takes and perspectives covering topics such as why he thinks there is no right way in venture (many paths work per him), why the venture industry would be better off with reduced fund sizes across the board, why he believes multistage investors investing at seed stage has corrupted the seed market, why not following on is a wrong strategy, why emerging markets investing is dead, why the cardinal mistake of investing in emerging markets is overpaying, why he thinks VC value add (beyond hiring help) is bullshit, his single biggest investing mistake (not exiting more positions via secondaries) and why GP commits are a big source of misalignment between LPs and GPs.
